ParlaMint-HR_2008-12-09-0.u22497	Mr. President, the Honorable Secretary of State, ladies and gentlemen who stayed at 22:22 p.m. Here is a brief version of this Proposal for a Law on Amendments and Supplements to the Law on State Support for Education and Education. In the beginning, I will say that the Club of Representatives of the HSS will support this bill, and with a brief explanation and reasons why we will do so. The rapid development of technology and the growing competition of economic operators in the international market require continuous education of workers. The Law on State support for education and training of workers prescribes financial support as tax relief for entrepreneurs who refer to their workers and entrepreneurs whose plants and workshops are trained in craftsmanship. However, as we have already heard the Law on State Support for Education and Education, it is necessary to comply with the provisions of the Income Tax Act, since, according to the Income Tax Act, the total annual income tax base is determined on the basis of income obtained by natural persons from non-permanent labour income, independent activity, property, property rights, capital, insurance, etc., that I do not list everything, and the aim of the proposer of this law is to provide tax relief for education and education for taxpayers who earn income only from independent activity. This means that, as a basis for the calculation of support for education and education for natural persons carrying out economic activity, income from independent activity should be determined, rather than the basis for the calculation of income tax. Furthermore, as the maximum possible amount of support that scientists can achieve, we have also heard this, only in percentage amount, the reduction of the base of the profit and income tax base, which is due to the high amount of profits generated by the largest commercial companies, the possible amount of support should be limited to the absolute amount, i.e. to the amount prescribed by Article 45(1). Income tax regulations and for the purpose of bringing support into real-life frameworks. This proposal and does so by prescribing Article 4 that the amount of the reduction in the base of profit or income tax from self-employed activity cannot be greater than the annual non-taxable amount, rewards to students during practical work and teaching by each scientist at the very time of teaching. Since the Government of the Republic of Croatia has made a decision on a map of regional support which sets out areas in which state support to economic development can be granted, it is also necessary to harmonise the provisions laid down in the State Support Act and for education and education by state support for education and education for users who carry out their activities in the area of the determined regional State support map. The proposed amendments go towards harmonising already adopted legal solutions, so that there is no difficulty in practice due to disagreements with applicable rules, i.e. that users may exercise their rights uninterruptedly. This appropriation is intended to cover: It is also important to align the provisions of the Law on State Support for Education and Education with the provisions of the decision on a map of regional support, in order to allow the use of an additional reduction of the basis for the calculation of income tax and income from the independent activities of entrepreneurs operating in the areas of central and eastern Croatia, the Adriatic Croatia and north-western Croatia. It is important to stress at the end of this short part of my debate on behalf of the club that it is not necessary to provide for specific funding from the state budget, and as regards urgency, it is very justified because it removes the difference in support between large and small entrepreneurs, but also because of the limited duration of the Amending and Supplemental Act on State Support for Education and Education. That's it. Thank you very much, Mr. Vice President, and good night.
